Schematic representation of a UV–Vis spectra of compounds 5f,g, 6a-g, 7a-g, 8a,b and the respective band gap energy estimation 
Based on each spectrum, the optical band gap can be estimated according to Eq. (1) [Costa J.C.S. at al., Optical Materials, 2016, 58, 51-60. 
https://doi.org/10.1016/j.optmat.2016.03.041]. Eopt represents the optical band gap expressed in eV and λa.e. denotes the absorption edge wavelength 
expressed in nm, obtained from the offset wavelength derived from the low energy absorption band as schematically represented below. 
𝐸𝑜𝑝𝑡 (𝑒𝑉) = ℎ × 𝑓= ℎ × 
𝑐

1240
